<!-- {"role": "assistant", "content": "<WriteFile>\n<fileName>src/notices_store.py</fileName>\n<contentOfFile>\"\"\"\nNotices Store Module\n====================\n\nHandles persistent storage and CRUD operations for notices.\nNotices are stored in ~/.config/KosDWM/notices.json\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport uuid\nimport threading\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom typing import Optional, List, Dict, Any\n\n\nclass Notice:\n    \"\"\"\n    Represents a single notice with all its properties.\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, \n                 title: str,\n                 content: str = \"\",\n                 due_date: Optional[datetime] = None,\n                 reminder_time: Optional[datetime] = None,\n                 priority: str = \"medium\",\n                 notice_id: Optional[str] = None,\n                 created_date: Optional[datetime] = None,\n                 completed: bool = False):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize a Notice.\n        \n        Args:\n            title: The notice title (required)\n            content: Detailed content/description\n            due_date: When the notice is due (optional)\n            reminder_time: When to show reminder (optional)\n            priority: 'low', 'medium', or 'high'\n            notice_id: Unique ID (auto-generated if not provided)\n            created_date: Creation timestamp (auto-generated if not provided)\n            completed: Whether the notice is completed\n        \"\"\"\n        self.id = notice_id or str(uuid.uuid4())\n        self.title = title\n        self.content = content\n        self.created_date = created_date or datetime.now()\n        self.due_date = due_date\n        self.reminder_time = reminder_time\n        self.priority = priority if priority in (\"low\", \"medium\", \"high\") else \"medium\"\n        self.completed = completed\n    \n    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Convert notice to dictionary for JSON serialization.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"id\": self.id,\n            \"title\": self.title,\n            \"content\": self.content,\n            \"created_date\": self.created_date.isoformat() if self.created_date else None,\n            \"due_date\": self.due_date.isoformat() if self.due_date else None,\n            \"reminder_time\": self.reminder_time.isoformat() if self.reminder_time else None,\n            \"priority\": self.priority,\n            \"completed\": self.completed\n        }\n    \n    @classmethod\n    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Notice':\n        \"\"\"Create Notice from dictionary.\"\"\"\n        return cls(\n            title=data.get(\"title\", \"\"),\n            content=data.get(\"content\", \"\"),\n            notice_id=data.get(\"id\"),\n            created_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data[\"created_date\"]) if data.get(\"created_date\") else None,\n            due_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data[\"due_date\"]) if data.get(\"due_date\") else None,\n            reminder_time=datetime.fromisoformat(data[\"reminder_time\"]) if data.get(\"reminder_time\") else None,\n            priority=data.get(\"priority\", \"medium\"),\n            completed=data.get(\"completed\", False)\n        )\n    \n    def is_overdue(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if notice is overdue.\"\"\"\n        if self.completed or not self.due_date:\n            return False\n        return datetime.now() > self.due_date\n    \n    def is_due_today(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if notice is due today.\"\"\"\n        if self.completed or not self.due_date:\n            return False\n        today = datetime.now().date()\n        return self.due_date.date() == today\n    \n    def is_upcoming(self, days: int = 7) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if notice is due within specified days.\"\"\"\n        if self.completed or not self.due_date:\n            return False\n        future = datetime.now() + timedelta(days=days)\n        return datetime.now() <= self.due_date <= future\n    \n    def is_reminder_due(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if reminder time has been reached.\"\"\"\n        if self.completed or not self.reminder_time:\n            return False\n        return datetime.now() >= self.reminder_time\n    \n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\n        return f\"Notice(id={self.id[:8]}, title='{self.title[:30]}', completed={self.completed})\"\n\n\nclass NoticesStore:\n    \"\"\"\n    Manages persistent storage of notices with thread-safe operations.\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self._data_path = Path.home() / \".config\" / \"KosDWM\" / \"notices.json\"\n        self._notices: Dict[str, Notice] = {}\n        self._lock = threading.RLock()\n        self._ensure_directory()\n        self._load()\n    \n    def _ensure_directory(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure the config directory exists.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            self._data_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        except IOError as e:\n            print(f\"Error creating notices directory: {e}\")\n    \n    def _load(self):\n        \"\"\"Load notices from JSON file.\"\"\"\n        if not self._data_path.exists():\n            self._notices = {}\n            return\n        \n        try:\n            with open(self._data_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                data = json.load(f)\n            \n            with self._lock:\n                self._notices = {}\n                for item in data.get(\"notices\", []):\n                    try:\n                        notice = Notice.from_dict(item)\n                        self._notices[notice.id] = notice\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        print(f\"Error loading notice: {e}\")\n            \n            print(f\"Loaded {len(self._notices)} notices\")\n            \n        except (json.JSONDecodeError, IOError) as e:\n            print(f\"Error loading notices file: {e}\")\n            self._notices = {}\n    \n    def _save(self):\n        \"\"\"Save notices to JSON file.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            with self._lock:\n                data = {\n                    \"notices\": [n.to_dict() for n in self._notices.values()],\n                    \"last_saved\": datetime.now().isoformat()\n                }\n            \n            with open(self._data_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n            \n        except IOError as e:\n            print(f\"Error saving notices: {e}\")\n            raise\n    \n    # CRUD Operations\n    \n    def create(self, title: str, **kwargs) -> Notice:\n        \"\"\"\n        Create a new notice.\n        \n        Args:\n            title: Notice title (required)\n            **kwargs: Other notice fields (content, due_date, reminder_time, priority)\n        \n        Returns:\n            The created Notice object\n        \"\"\"\n        if not title or not title.strip():\n            raise ValueError(\"Title cannot be empty\")\n        \n        notice = Notice(title=title.strip(), **kwargs)\n        \n        with self._lock:\n            self._notices[notice.id] = notice\n        \n        self._save()\n        return notice\n    \n    def get(self, notice_id: str) -> Optional[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get a notice by ID.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return self._notices.get(notice_id)\n    \n    def get_all(self) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get all notices.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return list(self._notices.values())\n    \n    def update(self, notice_id: str, **kwargs) -> Optional[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Update a notice.\n        \n        Args:\n            notice_id: ID of notice to update\n            **kwargs: Fields to update\n        \n        Returns:\n            Updated Notice or None if not found\n        \"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            notice = self._notices.get(notice_id)\n            if not notice:\n                return None\n            \n            for key, value in kwargs.items():\n                if hasattr(notice, key):\n                    setattr(notice, key, value)\n        \n        self._save()\n        return self.get(notice_id)\n    \n    def delete(self, notice_id: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Delete a notice.\n        \n        Returns:\n            True if deleted, False if not found\n        \"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if notice_id not in self._notices:\n                return False\n            del self._notices[notice_id]\n        \n        self._save()\n        return True\n    \n    def mark_completed(self, notice_id: str, completed: bool = True) -> Optional[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Mark a notice as completed or uncompleted.\"\"\"\n        return self.update(notice_id, completed=completed)\n    \n    # Query Operations\n    \n    def get_active(self) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get all non-completed notices.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self._notices.values() if not n.completed]\n    \n    def get_completed(self) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get all completed notices.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self._notices.values() if n.completed]\n    \n    def get_overdue(self) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get overdue notices.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self._notices.values() if n.is_overdue()]\n    \n    def get_due_today(self) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get notices due today.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self._notices.values() if n.is_due_today()]\n    \n    def get_upcoming(self, days: int = 7) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get notices due within specified days.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self._notices.values() if n.is_upcoming(days)]\n    \n    def get_by_priority(self, priority: str) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get notices by priority level.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self._notices.values() if n.priority == priority]\n    \n    def get_reminders_due(self) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"Get notices with reminders that are due.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self._notices.values() if n.is_reminder_due()]\n    \n    def search(self, query: str) -> List[Notice]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Search notices by title or content.\n        \n        Args:\n            query: Search string\n        \n        Returns:\n            List of matching notices\n        \"\"\"\n        query = query.lower()\n        with self._lock:\n            return [\n                n for n in self._notices.values()\n                if query in n.title.lower() or query in n.content.lower()\n            ]\n    \n    # Statistics\n    \n    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, int]:\n        \"\"\"Get statistics about notices.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            notices = list(self._notices.values())\n            return {\n                \"total\": len(notices),\n                \"active\": len([n for n in notices if not n.completed]),\n                \"completed\": len([n for n in notices if n.completed]),\n                \"overdue\": len([n for n in notices if n.is_overdue()]),\n                \"due_today\": len([n for n in notices if n.is_due_today()]),\n                \"high_priority\": len([n for n in notices if n.priority == \"high\" and not n.completed])\n            }\n    \n    def get_count(self) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Get total number of notices.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return len(self._notices)\n    \n    def get_active_count(self) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Get count of active (non-completed) notices.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return len([n for n in self._notices.values() if not n.completed])\n    \n    # Import/Export\n    \n    def export_to_json(self, file_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Export all notices to a JSON file.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            data = {\n                \"notices\": [n.to_dict() for n in self._notices.values()],\n                \"exported\": datetime.now().isoformat()\n            }\n        \n        with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n    \n    def import_from_json(self, file_path: str, merge: bool = False):\n        \"\"\"\n        Import notices from a JSON file.\n        \n        Args:\n            file_path: Path to JSON file\n            merge: If True, merge with existing; if False, replace all\n        \"\"\"\n        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n        \n        imported = []\n        for item in data.get(\"notices\", []):\n            try:\n                notice = Notice.from_dict(item)\n                # Generate new ID to avoid conflicts\n                notice.id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n                imported.append(notice)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error importing notice: {e}\")\n        \n        with self._lock:\n            if not merge:\n                self._notices.clear()\n            \n            for notice in imported:\n                self._notices[notice.id] = notice\n        \n        self._save()\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 316, "rowId": 27, "timestamp": 1784740808.8265398, "date": "2026-07-22", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 184339, content_label.pack(fill=tk.X)\n        \n        # Action buttons\n        btn_frame = tk.Frame(row, bg=bg_color)\n        btn_frame.pack(side=tk.RIGHT)\n        \n        if not notice.completed:\n \"\"\"Dialog for adding/editing notices.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent, store: NoticesStore, notice: Notice = None):\n        self.store = store\n        self.notice = notice\n _stop_api_server(self):\n        \"\"\"Stop the HTTP API server.\"\"\"\n        with self.api_lock:\n            if self.api_server:\n                self.api_server.stop()\n                self.api_running = False\n                print(\"Notices API stopped\")\n    \n    def _start_refresh_timer(self):\n        \"\"\"Start a timer to refresh the badge count periodically.\"\"\"\n        def refresh_loop():\n            while True:\n                time.sleep(60)  # Refresh every minute\n                if self.window and self.window.winfo_exists():\n                    try:\n \"\ud83d\udcdd\"\n    \n    def get_tooltip(self) -> str:\n        active = self.store.get_active_count()\n        overdue = len(self.store.get_overdue())\n        due_today = parts.append(f\"{due_today} due today\")\n        if active > 0:\n            parts.append(f\"{active} active\")\n        \n        api_status = \"API: ON\" if self.api_running else \"API: OFF\"\n   self.on_complete = on_complete\n        self.on_snooze = on_snooze\n        self.on_dismiss = on_dismiss\n        \n        self.window = None\n        self.remaining_time =    height = self.window.winfo_height()\n        x = (self.window.winfo_screenwidth() // 2) - (width // 2)\n        y = (self.window.winfo_screenheight() // 2) - (height // 2)\n        self.window.geometry(f'+{x}+{y}')\n        \n        # Header with icon\n        header = tk.Frame(self.window, bg='#4a90d9', padx=10, pady=10)\n        header.pack(fill=tk.X)\n        \n        tk.Label(\n            header,\n            text=\"\u23f0 Reminder\",\n            font=('Arial', 14, 'bold'),\n            fg='white',\n            bg='#4a90d9'\n        ).pack(side=tk.LEFT)\n        \n        # Auto-close timer label\n        self.timer_label = tk.Label(\n            header,\n            text=f\"Auto-close in {self.remaining_time}s\",\n            fg='white',\n            bg='#4a90d9',\n            font=('Arial', 9)\n        )\n        self.timer_label.pack(side=tk.RIGHT)\n        \n        # Content\n        content = tk.Frame(self.window, padx=20, pady=15)\n        content.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)\n        \n        tk.Label(\n            content,\n            text=self.title,\n            font=('Arial', 12, 'bold'),\n            wraplength=360\n        ).pack(anchor='w', pady=(0, 5))\n        \n        if self.message:\n            tk.Label(\n                content,\n                text=self.message,\n                font=('Arial', 10),\n                wraplength=360,\n                fg='gray'\n            ).pack(anchor='w')\n        \n        # Snooze options\n        snooze_frame = tk.LabelFrame(self.window, text=\"Snooze\", padx=10, pady=5)\n        snooze_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=20, pady=5)\n        \n        snooze_times = self.settings.get(\"snooze_times\", [5, 15, 30])\n        for minutes in snooze_times:\n            tk.Button(\n                snooze_frame,\n                text=f\"{minutes} min\",\n                command=lambda m=minutes: self._snooze(m),\n                width=8\n            ).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)\n        \n        # Action buttons\n        btn_frame = tk.Frame(self.window, pady=10)\n        btn_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=20)\n        \n        tk.Button(\n            btn_frame,\n            text=\"Dismiss\",\n            command=self._dismiss,\n            width=10\n        ).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)\n        \n        tk.Button(\n            btn_frame,\n Handle window close\n        self.window.protocol(\"WM_DELETE_WINDOW\", self._dismiss)\n        \n        # Start auto-close timer\n        self._start_timer()\n    \n    def _start_timer(self):\n        \"\"\"Start the auto-close countdown.\"\"\"\n        if self.remaining_time > 0:\n            self.timer_label.config(text=f\"Auto-close in {self.remaining_time}s\")\n            self.remaining_time -= 1\n            self.timer_id = self.window.after(1000, self._start_timer)\n        else:\n            self._dismiss()\n    \n    def _snooze(self, minutes: int):\n        \"\"\"Snooze the reminder.\"\"\"\n        if self.timer_id:\n            self.window.after_cancel(self.timer_id)\n        \n        self.window.destroy()\n        \n        if self.on_snooze:\n            self.on_snooze(self.notice_id, minutes)\n    \n    def _dismiss(self):\n        \"\"\"Dismiss the reminder.\"\"\"\n        if self.timer_id:\n            self.window.after_cancel(self.timer_id)\n        \n        self.window.destroy()\n        \n        if self.on_dismiss:\n            self.on_dismiss(self.notice_id)\n    \n    def _complete(self):\n        \"\"\"Mark the notice as complete.\"\"\"\n        if self.timer_id:\n            self.window.after_cancel(self.timer_id)\n        \n        self.window.destroy()\n        \n        if self.on_complete:\n            self.on_complete(self.notice_id)\n    \n    def close(self):\n        \"\"\"Force close the popup.\"\"\"\n        if self.window and self.window.winfo_exists():\n            if self.timer_id:\n                self.window.after_cancel(self.timer_id)\n            self.window.destroy()\n\n\nclass DismissedReminders:\n    \"\"\"\n    Tracks dismissed reminders to avoid duplicates.\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    DISMISSED_FILE = Path.home() / \".config\" / \"KosDWM\" / \"dismissed_reminders.json\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self._dismissed: Dict[str, str] = {}  # notice_id -> dismissed_time\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._ensure_directory()\n        self._load()\n        self._cleanup_old()\n    \n    def _ensure_directory(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure the config directory exists.\"\"\"\n        self.DISMISSED_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    \n    def _load(self):\n        \"\"\"Load dismissed reminders from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.DISMISSED_FILE.exists():\n            try:\n                with open(self.DISMISSED_FILE, 'r') as f:\n                    self._dismissed = json.load(f)\n            except (json.JSONDecodeError, IOError) as e:\n                print(f\"Error loading dismissed reminders: {e}\")\n                self._dismissed = {}\n    \n    def _save(self):\n        \"\"\"Save dismissed reminders to file.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            with open(self.DISMISSED_FILE, 'w') as f:\n                json.dump(self._dismissed, f, indent=2)\n        except IOError as e:\n            print(f\"Error saving dismissed reminders: {e}\")\n    \n    def _cleanup_old(self):\n        \"\"\"Remove entries older than 24 hours.\"\"\"\n        cutoff = (datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=24)).isoformat()\n        with self._lock:\n            self._dismissed = {\n                k: v for k, v in self._dismissed.items() \n                if v > cutoff\n            }\n            self._save()\n    \n    def is_dismissed(self, notice_id: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if a reminder has been dismissed.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return notice_id in self._dismissed\n    \n    def dismiss(self, notice_id: str):\n        \"\"\"Mark a reminder as dismissed.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            self._dismissed[notice_id] = datetime.now().isoformat()\n            self._save()\n    \n    def clear(self, notice_id: str):\n        \"\"\"Clear dismissed status for a reminder.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if notice_id in self._dismissed:\n                del self._dismissed[notice_id]\n                self._save()\n    \n    def clear_all(self):\n        \"\"\"Clear all dismissed reminders.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            self._dismissed.clear()\n            self._save()\n\n\nclass ReminderThread(threading.Thread):\n    \"\"\"\n    Background thread that checks for due reminders.\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, notices_store, \n                 on_reminder: Optional[Callable] = None,\n                 settings: Optional[ReminderSettings] = None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize the reminder thread.\n        \n        Args:\n            notices_store: NoticesStore instance\n            on_reminder: Callback when reminder is due (notice_id, title, content)\n settings or ReminderSettings()\n        self.dismissed = DismissedReminders()\n        \n        self._running = False\n        self._stop_event = threading.Event()\n        self._last_check_time = None\n        self._active_popups: Dict[str, NotificationPopup] = {}\n        self._popup_lock = threading.Lock()\n        \n        # Snoozed reminders: notice_id -> wake_time\n        self._snoozed: Dict[str, datetime] = {}\n        self._snooze_lock = threading.Lock()\n    \n    def run(self):\n        \"\"\"Main loop - check for reminders periodically.\"\"\"\n        self._running = True\n        print(\"ReminderThread started\")\n        \n        while not self._stop_event.is_set():\n            if self.settings.get(\"enabled\", True):\n                self._check_reminders()\n            \n            # Wait for check interval or until stopped\n            check_interval = self.settings.get(\"check_interval\", 60)\n            self._stop_event.wait(timeout=check_interval)\n        \n        self._running = False\n        print(\"ReminderThread stopped\")\n    \n    def stop(self):\n        \"\"\"Stop the reminder thread.\"\"\"\n        self._stop_event.set()\n        \n        # Close all active popups\n        with self._popup_lock:\n            for popup in list(self._active_popups.values()):\n                popup.close()\n            self._active_popups.clear()\n    \n    def _check_reminders(self):\n        \"\"\"Check for reminders that are due.\"\"\"\n        now = datetime.now()\n        \n        # Detect system time changes (backward)\n        if self._last_check_time and now < self._last_check_time:\n            print(\"System time change detected (backward), adjusting...\")\n            # Clear snoozed reminders that might be in the past\n            with self._snooze_lock:\n                self._snoozed = {\n                    k: v for k, v in self._snoozed.items() \n                    if v > now\n                }\n        \n        self._last_check_time = now\n        \n        # Get active notices with reminders\n        notices = self.store.get_active()\n        \n        for notice in notices:\n            # Skip if already has active popup\n            with self._popup_lock:\n                if notice.id in self._active_popups:\n                    continue\n            \n            # Skip if dismissed\n            if self.dismissed.is_dismissed(notice.id):\n                continue\n            \n            # Check if snoozed\n            with self._snooze_lock:\n                if notice.id in self._snoozed:\n                    if now < self._snoozed[notice.id]:\n                        continue\n                    else:\n                        # Snooze expired\n                        del self._snoozed[notice.id]\n            \n            # Check if reminder is due\n            if notice.reminder_time and now >= notice.reminder_time:\n                self._trigger_reminder(notice)\n    \n    def _trigger_reminder(self, notice):\n        \"\"\"Trigger a reminder notification.\"\"\"\n        print(f\"Reminder triggered: {notice.title}\")\n        \n        # Play sound if enabled\n        if self.settings.get(\"play_sound\", True):\n            self._play_sound()\n        \n        # Call callback if provided\n        if self.on_reminder:\n            self.on_reminder(notice.id, notice.title, notice.content)\n        \n        # Show visual alert in panel (callback to gadget)\n        # This will be handled by the callback\n    \n    def _play_sound(self):\n        \"\"\"Play notification sound.\"\"\"\n        sound_cmd = self.settings.get(\"sound_command\")\n        if sound_cmd and SOUND_AVAILABLE:\n            try:\n                subprocess.Popen(\n                    sound_cmd.split(),\n                    stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,\n                    stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL\n                )\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error playing sound: {e}\")\n    \n    def show_popup(self, notice_id: str, title: str, message: str, \n                   parent_window=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Show a notification popup for a reminder.\n        \n        Args:\n            notice_id: The notice ID\n            title: Notice title\n            message: Notice content\n            parent_window: Optional parent tkinter window\n        \"\"\"\n        # Check max notifications\n        with self._popup_lock:\n            if len(self._active_popups) >= self.settings.get(\"max_notifications\", 5):\n                # Remove oldest popup\n                oldest_id = next(iter(self._active_popups))\n                self._active_popups[oldest_id].close()\n                del self._active_popups[oldest_id]\n        \n        def on_complete(nid):\n            self.store.mark_completed(nid, True)\n            self.dismissed.dismiss(nid)\n            with self._popup_lock:\n                self._active_popups.pop(nid, None)\n        \n        def on_snooze(nid, minutes):\n            self.dismissed.dismiss(nid)\n            with self._snooze_lock:\n                self._snoozed[nid] = datetime.now() + timedelta(minutes=minutes)\n            with self._popup_lock:\n                self._active_popups.pop(nid, None)\n        \n        def on_dismiss(nid):\n            self.dismissed.dismiss(nid)\n            with self._popup_lock:\n                self._active_popups.pop(nid, None)\n        \n        # Create popup\n        popup = NotificationPopup(\n            notice_id=notice_id,\n            title=title,\n            message=message,\n            settings=self.settings,\n            on_complete=on_complete,\n            on_snooze=on_snooze,\n            on_dismiss=on_dismiss\n        )\n        \n        with self._popup_lock:\n            self._active_popups[notice_id] = popup\n    \n    def snooze(self, notice_id: str, minutes: int):\n        \"\"\"Manually snooze a reminder.\"\"\"\n        with self._snooze_lock:\n            self._snoozed[notice_id] = datetime.now() + timedelta(minutes=minutes)\n        self.dismissed.dismiss(notice_id)\n    \n    def clear_snooze(self, notice_id: str):\n        \"\"\"Clear snooze status for a reminder.\"\"\"\n        with self._snooze_lock:\n            self._snoozed.pop(notice_id, None)\n    \n    def is_snoozed(self, notice_id: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if a reminder is currently snoozed.\"\"\"\n        with self._snooze_lock:\n            return notice_id in self._snoozed\n    \n    def get_snooze_time(self, notice_id: str) -> Optional[datetime]:\n        \"\"\"Get when a snoozed reminder will wake up.\"\"\"\n        with self._snooze_lock:\n            return self._snoozed.get(notice_id)\n    \n    def get_active_popup_count(self) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Get number of currently active popup notifications.\"\"\"\n        with self._popup_lock:\n            # Clean up closed popups\n            self._active_popups = {\n                k: v for k, v in self._active_popups.items() \n                if v.window and v.window.winfo_exists()\n            }\n            return len(self._active_popups)\n    \n    def test_reminder(self, title: str = \"Test Reminder\", \n                     message: str = \"This is a test notification.\"):\n        \"\"\"Trigger a test reminder immediately.\"\"\"\n        test_id = f\"test_{int(time.time())}\"\n        self.show_popup(test_id, title, message)\n    \n    def force_check(self):\n        \"\"\"Force an immediate reminder check.\"\"\"\n        self._check_reminders()\n\n\nclass NotificationSettingsDialog:\n    \"\"\"\n    Dialog for configuring notification settings.\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent, settings: ReminderSettings):\n        self.settings = settings\n        self.result = False\n        \n        self.dialog = tk.Toplevel(parent)\n        self.dialog.title(\"Notification Settings\")\n        self.dialog.geometry(\"400x400\")\n        self.dialog.resizable(False, False)\n        self.dialog.transient(parent)\n        self.dialog.grab_set()\n        \n        self._create_form()\n        \n        parent.wait_window(self.dialog)\n    \n    def _create_form(self):\n        \"\"\"Create the settings form.\"\"\"\n        pad = {'padx': 20, 'pady': 10}\n        \n        # Enable notifications\n        self.enabled_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=self.settings.get(\"enabled\", True))\n        tk.Checkbutton(\n            self.dialog,\n            text=\"Enable notifications\",\n            variable=self.enabled_var\n        ).pack(anchor='w', **pad)\n        \n        # Play sound\n        self.sound_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=self.settings.get(\"play_sound\", True))\n        tk.Checkbutton(\n            self.dialog,\n            text=\"Play sound\",\n            variable=self.sound_var\n        ).pack(anchor='w', **pad)\n        \n        # Sound command\n        tk.Label(self.dialog, text=\"Sound command:\").pack(anchor='w', padx=20)\n        self.sound_cmd_var = tk.StringVar(value=self.settings.get(\"sound_command\", \"\"))\n        tk.Entry(self.dialog, textvariable=self.sound_cmd_var, width=40).pack(fill=tk.X, **pad)\n        \n        # Notification duration\n        duration_frame = tk.Frame(self.dialog)\n        duration_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, **pad)\n        \n        tk.Label(duration_frame, text=\"Notification duration (seconds):\").pack(side=tk.LEFT)\n        self.duration_var = tk.IntVar(value=self.settings.get(\"notification_duration\", 10))\n        tk.Spinbox(\n            duration_frame,\n            from_=5,\n            to=300,\n            increment=5,\n            textvariable=self.duration_var,\n            width=5\n        ).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=10)\n        \n        # Check interval\n        interval_frame = tk.Frame(self.dialog)\n        interval_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, **pad)\n        \n        tk.Label(interval_frame, text=\"Check interval (seconds):\").pack(side=tk.LEFT)\n        self.interval_var = tk.IntVar(value=self.settings.get(\"check_interval\", 60))\n        tk.Spinbox(\n            interval_frame,\n            from_=10,\n            to=300,\n            increment=10,\n            textvariable=self.interval_var,\n            width=5\n        ).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=10)\n        \n        # Max notifications\n        max_frame = tk.Frame(self.dialog)\n        max_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, **pad)\n        \n        tk.Label(max_frame, text=\"Max simultaneous notifications:\").pack(side=tk.LEFT)\n        self.max_var = tk.IntVar(value=self.settings.get(\"max_notifications\", 5))\n        tk.Spinbox(\n            max_frame,\n            from_=1,\n            to=10,\n            textvariable=self.max_var,\n            width=5\n        ).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=10)\n        \n        # Buttons\n        btn_frame = tk.Frame(self.dialog)\n        btn_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=20)\n        \n        tk.Button(\n            btn_frame,\n            text=\"Save\",\n            command=self._save,\n            bg='#4a90d9',\n            fg='white',\n            width=10\n        ).pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)\n        \n        tk.Button(\n            btn_frame,\n            text=\"Cancel\",\n            command=self.dialog.destroy,\n            width=10\n        ).pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)\n    \n    def _save(self):\n        \"\"\"Save the settings.\"\"\"\n        self.settings.update(\n            enabled=self.enabled_var.get(),\n            play_sound=self.sound_var.get(),\n self.api_running = False\n                print(\"Notices API stopped\")\n    \n    def _start_refresh_timer(self):\n        \"\"\"Start a timer to refresh the badge count periodically.\"\"\"\n \"\ud83d\udcdd\"\n    \n    def get_tooltip(self) -> str:\n        active = self.store.get_active_count()\n        overdue = len(self.store.get_overdue())\n        due_today = parts.append(f\"{due_today} due today\")\n        if active > 0:\n            parts.append(f\"{active} active\")\n        \n        api_status = \"API: ON\" if self.api_running else \"API: OFF\"\n        \n        if parts:\n            return f\"Notices ({api_status}): \" + \", \".join(parts)\n        return f\"Click to view notices ({api_status})\"\n    \n    def get_description(self) -> str:\n is None:\n                    self.api_server = NoticesAPIServer(\n                        store=self.store,\n                        port=self.API_PORT\n                    )\n                    self.api_running = self.api_server.start(threaded=True)\n                    if self.api_running:\n                        print(f\"Notices API started on port {self.API_PORT}\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Failed to start API server: {e}\")\n            self.api_running = False\n    \n    def _stop_api_server(self):\n        \"\"\"Stop the HTTP API server.\"\"\"\n def _start_reminder_thread(self):\n        \"\"\"Start the reminder notification thread.\"\"\"\n        if self.reminder_thread is None or not self.reminder_thread._running:\n            self.reminder_thread = ReminderThread(\n                notices_store=self.store,\n                on_reminder=self._on_reminder_triggered,\n                settings=self.reminder_settings\n            )\n            self.reminder_thread.start()\n            print(\"Reminder thread started\")\n    \n    def _stop_reminder_thread(self):\n        \"\"\"Stop the reminder notification thread.\"\"\"\n # Set visual alert\n        self.notification_alert = True\n        \n        # Show popup if window exists\n        if self.window and self.window.winfo_exists():\n            self.window.after(0, lambda: self._show_reminder_popup(\n                notice_id, title, content\n            ))\n    \n    def _show_reminder_popup(self, notice_id: str, title: str, content: str):\n        \"\"\"Show a      \"\"\"Start a timer to refresh the badge count periodically.\"\"\"\n        def refresh_loop():\n            while True:\n                time.sleep(60)  # Refresh every minute\n                if self.window and self.window.winfo_exists():\n                    try:\n                        self.window.after(0, self._refresh_list)\n                    except:\n                        pass\n        \n        self.refresh_timer = threading.Thread(target=refresh_loop, daemon=True)\n        self.refresh_timer.start()\n    \n    def get_name(self) -> str:\n        return \"notices\"\n    \n    def get_icon(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Return icon with badge count if there are active notices.\"\"\"\n        active_count = self.store.get_active_count()\n        \n        # Show alert \"\ud83d\udd14\"\n        \n        if active_count > 0:\n            return f\"\ud83d\udcdd{active_count}\"\n        return \"\ud83d\udcdd\"\n    \n    def get_tooltip(self) -> str:\n        active = parts.append(f\"{overdue} overdue\")\n        if due_today > 0:\n            parts.append(f\"{due_today} due today\")\n        if active > 0:\n            parts.append(f\"{active} active\")\n row.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=2, padx=2)\n        \n        # Priority indicator\n        priority_colors = {\"high\": \"\ud83d\udd34\", \"medium\": \"\ud83d\udfe1\", \"low\": \"\ud83d\udfe2\"}\n        priority_label = tk.Label(\n            row,\n            text=priority_colors.get(notice.priority, \"\u26aa\"),\n            bg=bg_color,\n            font=('Arial', 12)\n        )\n        priority_label.pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=(0, 5))\n        \n        # Title and content\n        text_frame = tk.Frame(row, bg=bg_color)\n        text_frame.pack(side=tk.LEFT, fill=tk.BOTH, text_frame,\n            text=title_text,\n            bg=bg_color,\n            fg=fg_color,\n            font=('Arial', 10, 'bold' if not notice.completed else ''),\n            anchor='w'\n        )\n        title_label.pack(fill=tk.X)\n        \n        # Due date info\n        date_text = \"\"\n        if notice.due_date:\n            days_until = (notice.due_date - datetime.now()).days\n            if days_until < 0:\n                date_text = f\"Overdue by {abs(days_until)} days\"\n            elif days_until == 0:\n                date_text = \"Due today\"\n            elif days_until ({notice.due_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')})\"\n        \n        # Show reminder info if set\n        if notice.reminder_time:\n            reminder_status = \"\"\n            if self.reminder_thread and \"\"\"Dialog for adding/editing notices.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent, store: NoticesStore, notice: Notice = None):\n        self.store = store\n        self.notice = notice\n        self.result = None\n        \n        self.dialog = tk.Toplevel(parent)\n        self.dialog.title(\"Edit Notice\" if notice else \"New Notice\")\n        self.dialog.geometry(\"400x350\")\n parent.wait_window(self.dialog)\n    \n    def _create_form(self):\n        \"\"\"Create the form fields.\"\"\"\n        pad = {'padx': 10, 'pady': 5}\n        \n        # Title\n        tk.Label(self.dialog, text=\"Title:\").pack(anchor='w', **pad)\n        self.title_var = tk.StringVar(value=self.notice.title if self.notice else \"\")\n        tk.Entry(self.dialog, tk.Label(self.dialog, text=\"Due Date (YYYY-MM-DD):\").pack(anchor='w', **pad)\n        due_date_str = \"\"\n        if self.notice and self.notice.due_date:\n            due_date_str = textvariable=self.reminder_var, width=40).pack(fill=tk.X, **pad)\n        \n        # Priority\n        tk.Label(self.dialog, text=\"Priority:\").pack(anchor='w', **pad)\n        self.priority_var = tk.StringVar(value=self.notice.priority if self.notice else \"medium\")\n        priority_frame = tk.Frame(self.dialog)\n        priority_frame.pack(anchor='w', **pad)\n        \n        for text, value in [(\"Low\", \"low\"), (\"Medium\", \"medium\"), (\"High\", \"high\")]:\n            tk.Radiobutton(\n                priority_frame,\n                text=text,\n btn_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, **pad)\n        \n        tk.Button(\n            btn_frame,\n            text=\"Save\",\n            command=self._save,\n            bg='#4a90d9',\n            fg='white',\n            width=10\n        ).pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)\n        \n        tk.Button(\n            btn_frame,\n            text=\"Cancel\",\n            command=self.dialog.destroy,\n            width=10\n        ).pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)\n    \n    def _save(self):\n        \"\"\"Save the notice.\"\"\"\n        title = self.title_var.get().strip()\n        if not title:\n            messagebox.showerror(\"Error\", \"Title is required\")\n            return\n        \n        content = self.content_var.get().strip()\n        \n        # Parse due date\n        due_date =  def _show_reminder_popup(self, notice_id: str, title: str, content: str):\n        \"\"\"Show a reminder popup notification.\"\"\"\n        if self.reminder_thread:\n Run tests\n    tests = [\n        test_data_model,\n        test_api_server,\n        test_notification_system,\n        test_data_persistence,\n        test_api_gui_sync,\n    ]\n    \n    for test_func in tests:\n        p, f = test_func()\n        total_passed += p\n        total_failed += f\n    \n    # <!-- {"role": "assistant", "content": "<WriteFile>\n<fileName>NOTICES_API_DOCUMENTATION.md</fileName>\n<contentOfFile># Notices HTTP API Documentation\n\n## Overview\n\nThe Notices HTTP API provides RESTful endpoints for managing notices/reminders programmatically. The API server runs on `localhost:5000` by default and supports CORS for cross-origin requests.\n\n## Base URL\n\n```\nhttp://localhost:5000/api\n```\n\n## Authentication\n\nCurrently, the API does not require authentication. It is intended for local use only.\n\n## Content Type\n\nAll requests and responses use JSON format:\n- Request: `Content-Type: application/json`\n- Response: `application/json`\n\n## Endpoints\n\n### 1. Health Check\n\n**GET** `/api/health`\n\nCheck if the API server is running.\n\n**Response:**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": true,\n  \"status\": \"healthy\",\n  \"timestamp\": \"2025-01-21T10:30:00\"\n}\n```\n\n**Example:**\n```bash\ncurl http://localhost:5000/api/health\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. List All Notices\n\n**GET** `/api/notices`\n\nRetrieve all notices with optional filtering and sorting.\n\n**Query Parameters:**\n\n| Parameter | Type | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| `completed` | boolean | Filter by completion status (`true` or `false`) |\n| `overdue` | boolean | Get only overdue notices (`true`) |\n| `due_today` | boolean | Get notices due today (`true`) |\n| `priority` | string | Filter by priority (`low`, `medium`, `high`) |\n| `search` | string | Search in title and content |\n\n**Response:**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": true,\n  \"count\": 3,\n  \"notices\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"uuid-here\",\n      \"title\": \"Team Meeting\",\n      \"content\": \"Weekly sync\",\n      \"created_date\": \"2025-01-20T09:00:00\",\n      \"due_date\": \"2025-01-21T14:00:00\",\n      \"reminder_time\": \"2025-01-21T13:30:00\",\n      \"priority\": \"high\",\n      \"completed\": false\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n**Examples:**\n\n```bash\n# Get all notices\ncurl http://localhost:5000/api/notices\n\n# Get active (non-completed) notices\ncurl \"http://localhost:5000/api/notices?completed=false\"\n\n# Get overdue notices\ncurl \"http://localhost:5000/api/notices?overdue=true\"\n\n# Get high priority notices\ncurl \"http://localhost:5000/api/notices?priority=high\"\n\n# Search notices\ncurl \"http://localhost:5000/api/notices?search=meeting\"\n\n# Combined filters\ncurl \"http://localhost:5000/api/notices?completed=false&priority=high\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 3. Get Single Notice\n\n**GET** `/api/notices/{id}`\n\nRetrieve a specific notice by ID.\n\n**Path Parameters:**\n\n| Parameter | Type | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| `id` | string | The notice UUID |\n\n**Response:**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": true,\n  \"notice\": {\n    \"id\": \"uuid-here\",\n    \"title\": \"Team Meeting\",\n    \"content\": \"Weekly sync\",\n    \"created_date\": \"2025-01-20T09:00:00\",\n    \"due_date\": \"2025-01-21T14:00:00\",\n    \"reminder_time\": \"2025-01-21T13:30:00\",\n    \"priority\": \"high\",\n    \"completed\": false\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Example:**\n```bash\ncurl http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000\n```\n\n**Error Response (404):**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": false,\n  \"error\": \"Not found\",\n  \"message\": \"Notice with ID 'xxx' not found\"\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 4. Create Notice\n\n**POST** `/api/notices`\n\nCreate a new notice.\n\n**Request Body:**\n\n| Field | Type | Required | Description |\n|-------|------|----------|-------------|\n| `title` | string | Yes | Notice title (1-200 characters) |\n| `content` | string | No | Detailed content |\n| `due_date` | string | No | Due date in `YYYY-MM-DD` format |\n| `reminder_time` | string | No | Reminder in `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM` format |\n| `priority` | string | No | Priority level: `low`, `medium` (default), `high` |\n\n**Request Example:**\n```json\n{\n  \"title\": \"Submit Report\",\n  \"content\": \"Quarterly sales report\",\n  \"due_date\": \"2025-01-25\",\n  \"reminder_time\": \"2025-01-25 09:00\",\n  \"priority\": \"high\"\n}\n```\n\n**Response (201 Created):**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": true,\n  \"message\": \"Notice created successfully\",\n  \"notice\": {\n    \"id\": \"new-uuid-here\",\n    \"title\": \"Submit Report\",\n    \"content\": \"Quarterly sales report\",\n    \"created_date\": \"2025-01-21T10:30:00\",\n    \"due_date\": \"2025-01-25T00:00:00\",\n    \"reminder_time\": \"2025-01-25T09:00:00\",\n    \"priority\": \"high\",\n    \"completed\": false\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Examples:**\n\n```bash\n# Simple notice\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/notices \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"title\": \"Buy groceries\"}'\n\n# Full notice with all fields\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/notices \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\n    \"title\": \"Doctor Appointment\",\n    \"content\": \"Annual checkup\",\n    \"due_date\": \"2025-02-15\",\n    \"reminder_time\": \"2025-02-15 08:00\",\n    \"priority\": \"high\"\n  }'\n```\n\n**Error Response (400):**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": false,\n  \"error\": \"Bad request\",\n  \"message\": \"Title is required\"\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Update Notice\n\n**PUT** `/api/notices/{id}`\n\nUpdate an existing notice. All fields are optional - only provided fields are updated.\n\n**Path Parameters:**\n\n| Parameter | Type | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| `id` | string | The notice UUID |\n\n**Request Body:**\n\nSame as Create Notice, but all fields are optional.\n\n**Request Example:**\n```json\n{\n  \"title\": \"Updated Title\",\n  \"priority\": \"medium\",\n  \"completed\": true\n}\n```\n\n**Response:**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": true,\n  \"message\": \"Notice updated successfully\",\n  \"notice\": {\n    \"id\": \"uuid-here\",\n    \"title\": \"Updated Title\",\n    \"content\": \"Original content\",\n    \"created_date\": \"2025-01-20T09:00:00\",\n    \"due_date\": \"2025-01-21T14:00:00\",\n    \"reminder_time\": \"2025-01-21T13:30:00\",\n    \"priority\": \"medium\",\n    \"completed\": true\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Examples:**\n\n```bash\n# Update title\ncurl -X PUT http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000 \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"title\": \"New Title\"}'\n\n# Mark as completed\ncurl -X PUT http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000 \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"completed\": true}'\n\n# Update due date\ncurl -X PUT http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000 \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"due_date\": \"2025-03-01\"}'\n\n# Clear due date\ncurl -X PUT http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000 \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"due_date\": null}'\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. Delete Notice\n\n**DELETE** `/api/notices/{id}`\n\nDelete a notice permanently.\n\n**Path Parameters:**\n\n| Parameter | Type | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| `id` | string | The notice UUID |\n\n**Response:**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": true,\n  \"message\": \"Notice deleted successfully\"\n}\n```\n\n**Example:**\n```bash\ncurl -X DELETE http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000\n```\n\n---\n\n### 7. Mark Notice as Complete\n\n**POST** `/api/notices/{id}/complete`\n\nToggle or set the completion status of a notice.\n\n**Path Parameters:**\n\n| Parameter | Type | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| `id` | string | The notice UUID |\n\n**Request Body (optional):**\n\n| Field | Type | Default | Description |\n|-------|------|---------|-------------|\n| `completed` | boolean | `true` | Set completion status |\n\n**Response:**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": true,\n  \"message\": \"Notice marked as completed\",\n  \"completed\": true\n}\n```\n\n**Examples:**\n\n```bash\n# Mark as completed (default)\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000/complete\n\n# Mark as completed explicitly\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000/complete \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"completed\": true}'\n\n# Mark as not completed (reopen)\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/notices/123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000/complete \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"completed\": false}'\n```\n\n---\n\n### 8. Get Statistics\n\n**GET** `/api/notices/stats`\n\nGet statistics about notices.\n\n**Response:**\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": true,\n  \"stats\": {\n    \"total\": 10,\n    \"active\": 7,\n    \"completed\": 3,\n    \"overdue\": 2,\n    \"due_today\": 1,\n    \"high_priority\": 3\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Example:**\n```bash\ncurl http://localhost:5000/api/notices/stats\n```\n\n---\n\n## Error Responses\n\n### HTTP Status Codes\n\n| Code | Meaning | Description |\n|------|---------|-------------|\n| 200 | OK | Request successful |\n| 201 | Created | Resource created successfully |\n| 400 | Bad Request | Invalid request data |\n| 404 | Not Found | Resource not found |\n| 405 | Method Not Allowed | HTTP method not supported |\n| 500 | Internal Server Error | Server error |\n\n### Error Response Format\n\n```json\n{\n  \"success\": false,\n  \"error\": \"Error type\",\n  \"message\": \"Human-readable error description\"\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Common Use Cases\n\n### Create a Quick Todo\n\n```bash\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/notices \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"title\": \"Call mom\"}'\n```\n\n### Create a Notice with Reminder\n\n```bash\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/notices \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\n    \"title\": \"Team Meeting\",\n    \"content\": \"Discuss Q1 roadmap\",\n    \"due_date\": \"2025-01-22\",\n    \"reminder_time\": \"2025-01-22 09:30\",\n    \"priority\": \"high\"\n  }'\n```\n\n### Get All Overdue Notices\n\n```bash\ncurl \"http://localhost:5000/api/notices?overdue=true\"\n```\n\n### Complete All Notices from API\n\n```bash\n# Get all active notices\ncurl -s \"http://localhost:5000/api/notices?completed=false\" | \\\n  jq -r '.notices[].id' | \\\n  while read id; do\n    curl -X POST \"http://localhost:5000/api/notices/$id/complete\"\n  done\n```\n\n### Export Notices\n\n```bash\ncurl -s http://localhost:5000/api/notices | jq '.notices' > notices_backup.json\n```\n\n### Import Notices (using store directly)\n\nSee `src/notices_store.py` `import_from_json()` method.\n\n---\n\n## Integration Examples\n\n### Python Script\n\n```python\nimport requests\n\nBASE_URL = \"http://localhost:5000/api\"\n\ndef create_notice(title, **kwargs):\n    data = {\"title\": title, **kwargs}\n    response = requests.post(f\"{BASE_URL}/notices\", json=data)\n    return response.json()\n\ndef get_overdue():\n    response = requests.get(f\"{BASE_URL}/notices?overdue=true\")\n    return response.json()[\"notices\"]\n\n# Create a notice\nresult = create_notice(\n    \"Project Deadline\",\n    due_date=\"2025-02-01\",\n    priority=\"high\"\n)\nprint(f\"Created: {result['notice']['id']}\")\n\n# Get overdue notices\noverdue = get_overdue()\nprint(f\"Overdue count: {len(overdue)}\")\n```\n\n### JavaScript/Fetch\n\n```javascript\nconst API_URL = 'http://localhost:5000/api';\n\nasync function createNotice(title, priority = 'medium') {\n  const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/notices`, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    headers: {\n      'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ title, priority }),\n  });\n  return response.json();\n}\n\nasync function getNotices() {\n  const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/notices`);\n  return response.json();\n}\n\n// Usage\ncreateNotice('Buy milk', 'low')\n  .then(data => console.log('Created:', data));\n```\n\n---\n\n## Notes\n\n- The API server runs in a background thread and shares the same data store as the GUI\n- Changes made via API are immediately visible in the GUI\n- The API is designed for local use and does not implement authentication\n- CORS is enabled for all origins (`*`) to allow browser-based clients\n- Date formats: `YYYY-MM-DD` for dates, `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM` for reminder times (24-hour format)\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### Connection Refused\n\n```bash\n# Check if server is running\ncurl http://localhost:5000/api/health\n\n# If not running, check the gadget is enabled\n```\n\n### Invalid Date Format\n\n```bash\n# Correct\n\"2025-01-21\"\n\"2025-01-21 14:30\"\n\n# Incorrect\n\"01/21/2025\"\n\"Jan 21, 2025\"\n\"2025-1-1\"\n```\n\n### Port Already in Use\n\nThe default port is 5000. If it's in use, you can modify `NoticesGadget.API_PORT` in the gadget code.\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 316, "rowId": 30, "timestamp": 1784741235.5825408, "date": "2026-07-22", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 57036, "response_tokens": 3394} -->

<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "\"\"\"\nKosDWM Gadget Example - Hello World\n====================================\n\nThis is an example gadget script for KosDWM.\nPlace this file in ~/.config/KosDWM/gadgets/ and it will be automatically discovered.\n\nTo create your own gadget:\n1. Copy this file and rename it (e.g., my_gadget.py)\n2. Change the class name and implement the required methods\n3. Restart KosDWM or click \"Reload Gadgets\" in the gadget configuration window\n\"\"\"\n\n# Required import - GadgetBase is the abstract base class all gadgets must extend\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Add parent directory to path to import GadgetBase\n# This allows the script to find src.gadgets when loaded dynamically\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / \"src\"))\n\nfrom gadgets import GadgetBase\nfrom tkinter import messagebox\n\n\nclass HelloWorldGadget(GadgetBase):\n    \"\"\"\n    A simple example gadget that shows a greeting message when clicked.\n    \n    All gadgets must extend GadgetBase and implement the following methods:\n    - get_name(): Returns a unique identifier for the gadget\n    - get_icon(): Returns the text/icon to display on the button\n    - get_tooltip(): Returns tooltip text shown on hover\n    - on_click(): Called when the gadget button is clicked\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Optional: Custom initialization.\n        You can add any setup code here (load settings, initialize state, etc.)\n        \"\"\"\n        super().__init__()\n        # Example: You could load custom settings here\n        self.click_count = 0\n    \n    def get_name(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Return the unique name/identifier of this gadget.\n        \n        This name is used:\n        - In the configuration file to track enabled/disabled state\n        - As a key in the gadget manager\n        \n        Must be unique across all gadgets!\n        \"\"\"\n        return \"hello_world\"\n    \n    def get_icon(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Return the text/icon to display on the gadget button.\n        \n        This can be:\n        - Text (like \"Hello\", \"CPU\", \"MEM\")\n        - Unicode symbols (like \"\ud83d\udd0b\", \"\ud83d\udcca\", \"\u23f0\")\n        - Short abbreviations (like \"HW\", \"SYS\")\n        \n        Keep it short - 4-6 characters recommended for best appearance.\n        \"\"\"\n        return \"Hello\"\n    \n    def get_tooltip(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Return the tooltip text shown when hovering over the gadget.\n        \n        This should briefly describe what the gadget does.\n        \"\"\"\n        return \"Click to see Hello World message\"\n    \n    def get_description(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Optional: Return a longer description for the configuration dialog.\n        \n        If not implemented, defaults to get_tooltip().\n        \"\"\"\n        return \"A simple example gadget that shows a greeting message and click counter.\"\n    \n    def on_click(self, event=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Handle click events on the gadget button.\n        \n        This is called when the user clicks the gadget in the panel.\n        You can perform any action here:\n        - Show a dialog/message\n        - Launch an application\n        - Toggle a setting\n        - Update system state\n        \n        Args:\n            event: The tkinter event object (can be ignored in most cases)\n        \"\"\"\n        self.click_count += 1\n        messagebox.showinfo(\n            \"Hello World\", \n            f\"Hello from KosDWM Gadget!\\n\\nYou've clicked this gadget {self.click_count} time(s).\"\n        )\n\n\n# Optional: You can define multiple gadgets in one file\n# class AnotherGadget(GadgetBase):\n#     def get_name(self) -> str:\n#         return \"another_gadget\"\n#     \n#     def get_icon(self) -> str:\n#         return \"Another\"\n#     \n#     def get_tooltip(self) -> str:\n#         return \"Another example gadget\"\n#     \n#     def on_click(self, event=None):\n#         messagebox.showinfo(\"Another\", \"This is another gadget!\")\n\n\n\"\"\"\nNOTES FOR GADGET DEVELOPERS:\n============================\n\n1. File Location:\n   - Place your .py file in ~/.config/KosDWM/gadgets/\n   - Files starting with _ are ignored\n\n2. Imports:\n   - You can import standard library modules freely\n   - For external packages, ensure they're installed system-wide\n   - To import from KosDWM's src directory, use the path manipulation shown above\n\n3. Multiple Gadgets:\n   - You can define multiple gadget classes in a single file\n   - Each will be discovered and registered separately\n\n4. State Persistence:\n   - If you need to save settings, save them to a file in ~/.config/KosDWM/\n   - The gadget instance is recreated each time the panel refreshes\n\n5. Error Handling:\n   - Wrap your on_click code in try-except blocks to prevent crashes\n   - Errors are logged to stdout/stderr\n\n6. UI Guidelines:\n   - Keep icons short (4-6 chars)\n   - Tooltips should be concise\n   - Avoid blocking operations in on_click (use threading if needed)\n\n7. Testing:\n   - After placing your file, click the \u2699 button in the panel\n   - Click \"Reload Gadgets\" to discover your new gadget\n   - Enable it and it should appear in the panel\n\nHappy gadget development!\n\"\"\"\n", "sessionId": 316, "rowId": 33, "timestamp": 1784742219.597602, "date": "2026-07-22", "name": "ReadFile"} -->
